Let's design strong local optimizer:

Problem: minimize φ(p)=2n max(h), p≥0, sum=1. We can normalize each step. A subgradient of max(h) at peak set top K is average of ∂h_k. We can do trust-region line search with multiplicative updates.

Fienup-style iteration:
Given current p, compute h=p*p. Desired h_target = some low-peak shape, e.g., h_target_k = C * min(h_k? or active) clipped at M. Use multiplicative correction in p via Fourier? Since p*p known, can update p spectrally: Let P = FFT(p). We want P^2 coefficients? P^2(z) not |P|^2, but we can treat polynomial: h = IFFT(P^2) (after appropriate length? For linear convolution, h = IFFT(P*P) if P length N). Desired h_desired. Then P_new = sqrt(h_desired_fft?) But P^2 (not conjugate) phase in frequency? This is like phase retrieval for polynomial square. We can update phase of P based on target h: P_new = P * (sqrt(FFT(h_target)/FFT(P^2)))? FFT(P^2) is pointwise product of DFT, but h_target coefficients -> desired P^2 frequency = FFT(h_target). Since P^2 coefficients are h; their DTFT is P(θ)^2. Therefore we can directly iteratively adjust P(θ) to sqrt(FFT(h_target))! This is key.

For finite linear convolution of length n, h_k = coeff of P(z)^2 where P(z)=∑_{i=0}^{n-1} p_i z^i. If we use a large FFT length N ≥2n-1, then P_N = FFT(p padded), and P_N.^2 inverse gives h (cyclic approximates linear if N≥2n-1). Indeed cyclic convolution of length N with N≥2n-1 gives linear conv. So h = IDFT(P_N^2) (no conj). Then if we choose a target h_target (nonnegative coefficients sum maybe 1), we can set P_target(θ) = sqrt(FFT(h_target)(θ))? But we need P(θ)^2 = desired frequency. Since P(θ)^2 in complex plane; square root can have phase issues. But we can update P multiplicatively: P_new = P * sqrt(FFT(h_target)/FFT(P^2)) with phase chosen to keep coefficients p nonnegative? The ratio magnitude adjusts frequency, phase? If ratio complex, multiplying P by sqrt of complex ratio changes phase of P, which changes coefficients. This is akin to phase retrieval for square root of spectrum. Simpler: Take P_new = phase(P) * sqrt(|FFT(h_target)|?) But P(θ)^2 should not only have magnitude but phase zero for real? For real symmetric p, P(θ) real even, P^2 nonnegative. But p nonnegative not symmetric, P complex; P^2 has phase 2 arg P, not arbitrary. The coefficients h are real nonnegative, so FFT(h) is real but can be negative if h not positive definite? h=p*p (convolution, not autocorr) is real nonnegative sequence, its FFT can be negative. P(θ)^2 = FFT(h)(θ), so P(θ) is a square root of this real function; P can be complex with arg P = 1/2 arg(FFT(h)) where sign of real function determines ±π/2. This is a nontrivial factorization. The linear convolution h is not positive definite, so FFT(h) can be negative. However P(θ)^2 for real P can be any real with zeros? P complex on circle.

But Fienup can be adapted: We desire h_target; given P, compute current h. In Fourier domain, P^2 vs desired H_des=FFT(h_target). We can update P by setting P_new's square closer: P_new = P * (G/G_cur)^{1/2} (where G=H_des, G_cur=FFT(P^2)=FFT(h)). Use principal sqrt. Then inverse FFT take first n coefficients, clip nonnegative, normalize. This is like sandwich: time domain nonnegative/length, frequency domain square spectrum. Since h_target nonnegative but its FFT may be negative, sqrt of negative has i; P phase changes. Might be effective.

We can choose h_target analytically: a low-peak sequence (nonnegative, sum1) that is feasible-ish. For example, take current h and clip peaks slightly: h_target = h with max reduced: h_target_k = max(C, h_k) or water-level. Then iteratively push p to achieve flatter h. This is the "projection" method: desired h = water_filling(h, max=M) with sum1. Then spectral update to make p*p ≈ h_target, then project p≥0, normalize. Repeat. This directly targets max reduction, unlike flatten spectrum. This seems promising and efficient O(N log N) per iteration.

Let's detail:
- Let N = next power of 2 >= 2n-1 (for linear convolution) or maybe >=2n for margins.
- p length n, sum1. Compute P = rfft? P^2 needs full complex; use fft length N. H = ifft(P*P) (complex imag tiny) length N; linear first L=2n-1 are h. (Because N≥L, no wrap.)

This direct h-target method seems powerful and different from previous. It directly controls convolution coefficients. We can implement a loop:
```
def h_target_iteration(p, n_iter, N, alpha, target_reduction, beta_schedule):
   p norm1
   for it: 
      P=fft(p,N); Hcur=fft(p*p)
      h=irfft? ifft(Hcur)[:L] real
      compute h_t target based on h
      G=fft(h_t, N)
      ratio = safe(G/Hcur)
      Pnew = P * pow(ratio, step)  # step=0.25? complex power using log
      pnew = ifft(Pnew)[:n].real
      pnew=clip(min(pnew,?); normalize; if score improved accept else reduce step
```

SCS algorithm flow:
```
def spectral_shape(p, iters, start_step, min_step, target_reduction, bfresh):
  n=len(p); N=nextpow2(2n-1)
  best=p; bestsc=score(p)
  step=0.2
  for it:
    P=fft(p,N); H=P*P; h=ifft(H).real[:L]
    M=h.max()
    # target max maybe = bestM*(1-tol) or M*(1-0.002)
    # construct ht
    ht = make_target(h, M)
    G=fft(ht,N)
    ratio=G/H_safe
    ...
    q=ifft(P_new).real
    p_new=q[:n]
    p_new=clip(p_new,0,?)
    normalize
    sc_new=score(p_new)
    if sc_new<bestsc - 1e-12: accept; step*=1.02 (up to 0.7)
    else: reject; step*=0.7
  return best
```

Let's think of solving the continuous/discrete problem via convex optimization over h and p with F ice regularization. Since p*p is quadratic, but the minimization of L∞ can be approximated by minimizing L2 norm of (p*p - target) subject to p≥0. This is a quartic problem. Let's design target construction more concretely:
Function `target_from_h(h, current_max, reduction)`:
- Work on `h` length L, sum approx1.
- We want target max `M_t` below current by at least `min_red` (e.g., 0.1%) and not too low.
- Approach: Soft clip at cap `c`. We can binary search cap in [0, current_max] to achieve after normalization max <= current_max, but maybe max = desired. We'll compute for a series of cap values and choose one that gives max target <= current_max and as low as possible but close to current (to maintain feasibility).
  For a given cap c: ht = min(h,c); S=ht.sum(); if S<=0 fail; max_t = c/S if c>0 else max. If max_t <= current_max * (1 - desired_red), choose. To find lowest cap feasible: maximize? Actually smaller cap -> S smaller, c/S increases, may exceed. There may be a cap interval where after normalization max <= current. We want the cap that gives target max as low as possible while max <= current. Since if too small cap, normalization blows max. The minimum possible target max via capping likely at some c. We can search c over quantiles.
- If no c yields reduction, use blend with uniform or `h_ref`. But maybe cap near current_max with tiny reduction works.

Actually if we set ht=min(h,c), max_t=c/S. If c close to current max, S ≈1 - total_overpeak*ε, max_t≈c/(1-A). For reduction, need c < current*(1-A). Total overpeak mass at new threshold. Let's implement binary:
```
def make_target(h, cur_max, red=0.002, min_cap_frac=0.5):
    L=h.size; target_max=cur_max*(1-red)
    # Precompute sorted h to find S(c) quickly.
    vals = np.sort(h)
    cmax=vals[-1]; cmin=0.5*cmax maybe?
    # We need max(c/S(c)) <= target_max.
    # S(c)=c*count_above? can compute direct for candidate caps.
    candidates = np.unique(np.concatenate([np.quantile(h, np.linspace(0.5,0.99999,50)), [target_max]]))
    best=None
    for c in candidates:
       ht=np.minimum(h,c); S=ht.sum()
       if S<=0: continue
       mt=max(c, np.max(ht)/S) # If c below all? Actually max ht=c if any capped else max h/S.
       if mt <= target_max*1.0001:
           # feasible; choose one with lowest mt? maybe maximum? 
           if best is None or mt<bestmt: best=ht.copy(); bestmt=mt
    if best is None:
       # blend with flat over L or with h itself
       flat=np.ones(L)/L
       # choose alpha to reduce max: blend=(1-α)h+αflat, max solve
       α values, pick first with max<cur_max
       best=...
    # ensure nonneg, sum1
    return best/S
```

Let's outline SCS function:
```
def spectral_shape_guide(a, iters, ref, target_frac=0.999, s_init=0.35, s_min=0.005):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1; N=1<<((2*n-2).bit_length())
   a=a/a.sum()
   best=a; best_sc=compute_score(a); best_M=...
   s= s_init
   α = 0.01
   for it in range:
      P=rfft(a,N)
      H=P*P
      h=irfft(H,N)[:L]
      # real max
      M=h.max()
      if 2n*M/S^2 < best_sc: update
      # find alpha
      for alpha in sorted alphas:
         ht=(1-alpha)*h + alpha*ref
         if ht.min()<0: continue
         if ht.sum()<=0: continue
         mt=ht.max()/ht.sum()
         if mt < best_M*0.999 or mt < M*0.999:
             break
      # if no alpha, use cap? maybe alpha=0.02
      ht/=ht.sum()
      G=rfft(ht,N)
      # adjust G[0]=1, G[nyq]=max(0,G[nyq])
      ...
      ratio_update...
      a_new=irfft(P_new,N)[:n]
      a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0)
      S=sum; if S<eps reject
      a_new/=S
      sc_new=compute_score(a_new)
      if sc_new < best_sc: accept best, best_M; s*=1.05; α*=? maybe increase
      else: reject; s*=0.8; α*=0.8
   return best
```
But note if we reject a_new, we keep old a and old P; next loop recomputes. Good.

def spectral_conv_refine(a, time_limit, ref, iters_cap, s_init=0.25, alpha_init=0.005):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1; N=nextpow2(L)
    a=a.copy(); a=np.maximum(a,0); a/=a.sum()
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a); best_M=...
    s=s_init; alpha=alpha_init
    no_improve=0
    for it in cap:
       if time out break
       P=rfft(a,N)
       H=P*P
       h=irfft(H,N)[:L]
       h=h.real; h=np.clip(h,0,None)
       # normalize sum to 1 to remove numerical
       hsum=h.sum(); if hsum>0: h/=hsum
       M=h.max()
       sc=2n*M
       if sc<best_sc: accept; no_improve=0; maybe anneal alpha/s
       else: no_improve++
       # choose alpha
       target_reft = ref # length L sum1, max_ref low
       # Try alphas progressive
       max_allowed = min(best_M*0.999, M*0.999) # target less than best? use best_M
       chosen_alpha=alpha
       for cand in [alpha, min(1,alpha*2), 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5]:
           ht = (1-cand)*h + cand*ref
           if ht.sum()<=0: continue
           mt=ht.max()/ht.sum()
           if mt <= max_allowed:
               chosen_alpha=cand; break
       # if none, use cap? or just alpha=0.01
       ht=(1-chosen_alpha)*h + chosen_alpha*ref
       ht=np.clip(ht,0,None); ht/=ht.sum()
       # ensure G0=1; Gnyq nonneg
       G=rfft(ht,N)
       if N%2==0:
           if G[N//2].real <0: G[N//2]=abs(G[N//2].real) # or 0
       G[0]=1.0+0j? But rfft returns real first. G[0]=ht.sum()=1.
       # Compute update
       Habs=np.abs(H); Gabs=np.abs(G)
       eps=1e-12*max(1.0, np.max(Gabs))
       # endpoint 0 and nyq handle real
       P_new=P.copy()
       # For non-endpoints:
       start=1
       end=(N//2) if N%2==0 else (N//2)
       # rfft length includes index N/2 if N even; handle separately.
       mask = Habs[start:nyq_start] > eps
       ...
```

Let's plan final program structure:
```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _nxt(n): return 1 << (int(n)-1).bit_length()

def _score_np(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size; if n==0 return inf
    L=2*n-1; N=_nxt(L)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); H=A*A; h=np.fft.irfft(H,N)[:L]; S=a.sum()
    if S<=1e-14: return inf
    return 2.0*n*float(h.max())/(S*S)
```
Since we normalize mostly, but keep.

`_norm(a)`: clip nonneg, min cap, sum ->1 or 0.

`_interp_to_length(a, m, rng=None)`: if m same return; if m > len: linear interpolation on [0,1] (preserve endpoints?); if m < len: average blocks or interp down. Use `np.interp` with x_new. For downsample, maybe average with reshape? Interp may smooth. For larger, add small noise optional.

`_initial_two_level(n, frac, d, rng)`: p first frac high A=1+d, rest B=1-d normalized? Since sum should n? Use p = np.ones(n); p[:k]=A; p[k:]=B; normalize. Ensure nonneg (d<=1). d=1/3 maybe. Add small noise. Also reverse option (high right) by frac=1-frac? Actually high right p[:]=low, p[k:]=high.

Loop details:
```
def spectral_refine(a, time_budget, ref, label):
   a = _norm(a); n=len(a); L=2n-1; N=...
   best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a); best_M=best_sc/(2n)
   a_cur=a.copy()
   P = rfft(a_cur,N); H=P*P; M=...
   s=0.3; alpha=0.02; no=0
   max_no=50
   for it in range(...):
      if time.monotonic() > t_end: break
      # current h from P/H
      h=irfft(H,N)[:L].real; h=np.clip(h,0,None); hsum=h.sum(); if hsum<=0 break; h/=hsum; M=h.max()
      sc=2n*M
      if sc < best_sc - 1e-12: best=...
      # target: try find alpha
      target_limit = min(best_sc, 2n*M)/(2n) * (1 - 0.0002) maybe use M
      alpha_current=alpha
      ht=None; mt=None
      for al in (alpha_current, min(1,alpha_current*2), 0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4):
          ht=(1-al)*h + al*ref
          if ht.min()<0: continue
          hts=ht.sum(); if hts<=0: continue
          mt=ht.max()/hts
          if mt <= M * (1 - 0.0005):
             break
      if ht is None: use cap maybe
      else ht/=hts
      if mt > M*0.9999: # no reduction, use cap
          cap = M*0.998
          ht2=np.minimum(h,cap)
          if ht2.sum()>0 and (ht2.max()/ht2.sum() <= M): ht=ht2/ht2.sum()
          else: ht=h # no target change
      G=rfft(ht,N)
      # adjust G
      if G[0].real != 1: G[0]=1+0j? but rfft[0] real; set.
      if N%2==0:
          ny=G[N//2]
          if not np.isfinite(ny): ...
          if ny.real < 0: G[N//2]=abs(ny.real) # maybe set positive
      # compute target square root
      X_t = np.zeros_like(P)
      X_t[0]=1.0
      for k in range(1, N//2 (+1)):
         gk=G[k]; mag=np.sqrt(abs(gk))
         ph=0.5*np.angle(gk)
         tk = mag*np.exp(1j*ph)
         # choose sign to align with P[k]
         if abs(P[k]) > 1e-12:
             if np.real(tk * np.conj(P[k])) < 0: tk = -tk
         X_t[k]=tk
      # For nyq, real: if N even, handle separately with sign
      if N%2==0:
         gny=G[N//2].real
         if gny >=0: sqrt=np.sqrt(gny); if P[N//2] <0: sqrt=-sqrt; X_t[N//2]=sqrt
         else: X_t[N//2]=0.0  # or sign*sqrt(abs)? not real
      # blend
      P_new = (1-s)*P + s*X_t  # or power? linear
      # Maybe ensure P_new[0]=1 (sum) and nyq real
      P_new[0]=1.0
      if N%2==0: P_new[N//2]=float(np.real(P_new[N//2]))
      q=irfft(P_new,N)[:n]
      a_new=np.clip(q,0,None)
      S=a_new.sum(); if S<1e-8: reject
      a_new/=S
      sc_new=_score(a_new) # use score function, maybe FFT size N already? If use _score it recomputes; okay. Could compute with P? a_new not P_new after clipping, so recompute.
      if sc_new < best_sc + 1e-6: # accept annealing
          a_cur=a_new; P=rfft(a_cur,N); H=P*P
          if sc_new < best_sc: best...
          no=0; s=min(0.8,s*1.03); alpha=min(0.6, alpha*1.05)
      else:
          no+=1; s=max(0.03, s*0.85); alpha=max(0.001, alpha*0.9)
          if no>100: # reset current to best? 
             a_cur=best.copy(); P=...; s=...; no=0
   return best,best_sc
```

   n=len(a)

Polish function:

def polish_lbfgs(a, time_budget):
   a=_norm(a); n=len(a); best=a; best_sc=_score(a)
   x=np.log(best+1e-300)
   betas=[100,500,2000,10000]
   if n>8192 betas=[500,2000]
   for beta in betas:
      if time monotonic > t_end: break
      remaining = t_end - time
      maxiter = int(min(3000, max(200, remaining*5 if n<4096 else remaining*1)))
      res = minimize(_lse_fg, x, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=...)
      p=softmax(res.x); sc=_score(p); if sc<best_sc: update
   return best,best_sc
```

Now, `_make_ref(L, limit, phase)` implementation:
```
def _make_ref(L, limit, phase):
    x=np.arange(L, dtype=float)/L
    # combine sin and maybe small linear to break more
    shape = np.sin(2*np.pi*x + phase)
    # maybe add 0.2*cos? Keep mean zero
    # Want max ref <= limit. Start amp 0.3
    for amp in [0.4,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.0]:
        base=1.0 + amp*shape
        if base.min() <= 0:
            amp *= 0.7
            base=1.0+amp*shape
        ref=base/base.sum()
        if ref.max() <= limit:
            return ref
    return np.ones(L)/L
```
But if limit<1/L, impossible; return uniform. Our limits >1/L (since best_M≈0.75/n >1/(2n)), uniform ok.

   while time < t_end and it<20000:
      h_norm = h.sum(); if h_norm>0: h/=h_norm
      M=h.max(); sc=2n*M
      if sc<best_sc ...
      # target blend: use fixed target with alpha

def coordinate_shave(a, time_budget, label=""):
  a=np.array(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0); S=sum; if S<=0 return
  L=2n-1; h=_linear_conv_fftp? Use FFT once.
  best=a.copy(); best_sc=score_from_h
  t_end=...
  no=0
  factors=(0.8,0.9,1.1,1.2,0.7,1.3)
  while time and no<max_no:
     hmax=h.max(); sc=2n*hmax/(S*S)
     if sc<best_sc: best=..., no=0 else no++
     # if no>max break
     # active peaks
     if hmax <=0: break
     active=np.argsort(h)[::-1][:6]  # full argsort L~16k ok each sweep
     progress=False
     for k in active:
        if time out break
        j_start=...; mlen=...
        if mlen<=0 continue
        js=np.arange(j_start,j_end+1)
        deriv=a[k-js]
        top_count=min(10,mlen)
        idx=np.argpartition(deriv, -top_count)[-top_count:]
        idx=idx[np.argsort(deriv[idx])[::-1]]
        for pos in idx:
           j=int(js[pos]); old=a[j]
           if old<=1e-14: continue
           best_f=None; best_sc_j=sc; best_slice=None? We can try multipliers, track best.
           for f in factors:
              new=old*f
              if new<0: continue
              # update h copy? Use old_slice copy
              sl=slice(j,2*n-1-j)
              old_slice=h[sl].copy()
              # apply
              delta=new-old
              h[sl] += 2*delta*a[sl-j]   # sl-j array same length
              # center correction
              h[sl][j] += delta*delta  # because sl-j at offset j is old
              Snew=S+delta
              if Snew<=1e-12:
                  h[sl]=old_slice; continue
              scj=2*n*float(h.max())/(Snew*Snew)
              if scj < best_sc_j:
                  best_sc_j=scj; best_new=new; best_delta=delta? 
              h[sl]=old_slice
           if best_new is not None:
              # apply best
              sl=...; h[sl] += ...; S=best_Snew; a[j]=best_new; sc=best_sc_j; progress=True
           # if time break
     if not progress: maybe no++ or relax? 
  # normalize best? Since S changed, normalize a_best (score invariant). Return best_a,best_sc
```

```
def _flat_reference(L, c, phase):
    if c <= 1.001: return ones/L
    x=np.arange(L)/L
    shape=np.sin(2*np.pi*x + phase)
    shape -= shape.mean()
    posmax=shape.max(); negmin=shape.min()
    # scale so positive max maybe 1, negative min maybe min_neg_scaled
    scale = 1.0/posmax if posmax>0 else 1
    shape_scaled = shape*scale
    # Now max=1, min = negmin*scale <= -1 maybe
    amp = min(c-1.0, -1.0/shape_scaled.min() if shape_scaled.min()<0 else 1e9)
    base=1+amp*shape_scaled
    if base.min()<=0: amp*=0.9; base=...
    ref=base/base.sum()
    # if ref.max() not <= c/L*1.05 maybe reduce amp further
    for _ in range(20):
        if ref.max() <= c/float(L)*1.02: break
        amp *= 0.9; if amp<0: return uniform
        base=1+amp*shape_scaled; base=np.clip(base,1e-12,None); ref=base/base.sum()
    return ref
```
Mean of shape after removing mean: max ≈1/√2, min≈-1/√2; scaling to max1 gives min -1. Good. Sum base=L. Max =1+amp =c if sin positive max at included point. Good.

```
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _next_pow2(m):
    m=int(m)
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def _norm(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return a
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-300:
        a=np.ones(a.size)/a.size
    else:
        a=a/s
    return a
```
Need not clip to1000? We return normalized <=1, fine. If input parent has values clipped 1000, normalize.

`_score_P`:
```
def _score_P(a,N):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size; S=float(a.sum())
    if S<=1e-300: return np.inf, None,None,None
    nL=2*n-1
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    H=A*A
    h=np.fft.irfft(H,N)[:nL]
    h=np.real(h)
    # h may have small negative due numerical; max should positive
    hmax=float(np.max(h))
    if hmax<0: hmax=0.0
    sc=2.0*n*hmax/(S*S)
    return sc,A,H,h
```
No clip negatives for max? h should nonnegative for nonnegative a; numerical may. Max okay. For target h, clip later.

def _convolve_self(a):

`_interp_to_length(a,m)`:

def _interp_to_length(a,m):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); m=max(2,int(m))
    if a.size==m: return _norm(a)
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(m)/m
    # Use linspace in [0,1]. For downsample, interpolation with endpoints maybe okay. To reduce artifacts, if m<a.size, average in blocks? Let's do if m<a.size: block average.
    if m < a.size:
       # pad to multiple
       rem=a.size - (a.size//m)*m? Simpler interpolation.
    xold=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,a.size)
    xnew=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    b=np.interp(xnew,xold,a)
    return _norm(b)
```
Use interpolation for all.

Spectral project code detailed:
```
def _spectral_project(a, time_budget, phase, min_c=1.05, s_init=0.3, alpha_init=0.15, label=""):
    a=_norm(a); n=a.size; L=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(L)
    shape=_shape_array(L, phase)
    best=a.copy(); sc,P0,H0,h0=_score_P(a,N); best_sc=sc
    a_cur=a.copy(); P=P0.copy(); H=H0; h=h0.copy()
    s=s_init; alpha=alpha_init; no=0; it=0; t_end=time.monotonic()+time_budget
    c_target=min(2.0, max(min_c, best_sc*0.995 if best_sc<np.inf else 1.8))
    target=_flat_reference_from_shape(L, shape, c_target)
    max_iters=max(100, int(time_budget*50))  # maybe
    while time.monotonic()<t_end and it<max_iters:
        hsum=h.sum()
        if hsum<=1e-20: break
        h=hsum normalized? h = h / hsum? Since sum should1; do if abs not: h = h/hsum
        M=float(np.max(h))
        sc=2*n*M  # S=1
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc=sc; best=a_cur.copy()
        # if target not lowering, adapt c_target
        tmax=float(target.max())
        if tmax >= M * 0.999:
            new_c = max(min_c, c_target - 0.02)
            if new_c == c_target and c_target <= min_c+1e-9:
                ht = None # use cap
            else:
                c_target = new_c
                target = _flat_reference_from_shape(L, shape, c_target)
                tmax = float(target.max())
        if tmax < M * 0.999:
            # choose alpha maybe adjust
            # ensure ht max < M
            ht = (1.0-alpha)*h + alpha*target
            # if ht.max() >= M*0.9999, increase alpha
            if ht.max() >= M*0.9999:
                # try larger alpha
                for al in [max(0.5,alpha), 0.5, 0.8, 1.0]:
                    ht2=(1.0-al)*h+al*target
                    if ht2.max() < M*0.9999:
                        alpha=al; ht=ht2; break
        else:
            # cap target
            cap = M*0.998
            ht = np.minimum(h, cap)
            Sht=ht.sum()
            if Sht<=0 or (ht.max()/Sht) >= M*0.9999:
                # blend with uniform
                ht = 0.5*h + 0.5*(np.ones(L)/L)
                if ht.max() >= M*0.9999:
                    # no safe target, break or use h
                    ht = h
            else:
                ht=ht/Sht
        # ht sum normalize
        ht=np.clip(ht,0.0,None); sh=ht.sum(); if sh<=0 break; ht/=sh
        G=np.fft.rfft(ht,N)
        G[0]=1.0+0j? rfft returns real; set G[0].real=1?
```
`np.fft.rfft` returns complex for positive, first is complex? It is complex with 0 imag. Setting `G[0] = 1.0 + 0j` okay. For Nyquist if N even:
```
if N % 2 == 0:
    nyq = G[N//2]
    if nyq.real < 0:
       G[N//2] = 0.0 + 0j  # maybe preserve magnitude? set to abs? target infeasible
       # But if set 0, target altered; okay.
```
But if G_nyq negative large, setting 0 might reduce target feasible. Maybe set to abs(nyq.real)? The necessary condition is nonnegative; choose 0 to not force high alternating. But 0 may be too strong. We'll set to max(0.0, nyq.real) (keep if positive). For phase root, if zero target P_nyq zero.
```
        # build Xt
        Xt=np.empty_like(P)
        Xt[0]=1.0+0j
        if N%2==0: Xt[N//2]=0.0
        half = N//2
        for? We need vectorized.
        Gabs=np.abs(G); H maybe not needed.
        mag=np.sqrt(Gabs)
        ang=0.5*np.angle(G)
        Xt = mag*np.exp(1j*ang)
        # choose sign with P
        dot = Xt*np.conj(P)
        flip = np.real(dot) < 0
        Xt[flip] *= -1
        # endpoints:
        Xt[0]=1.0
        if N%2==0:
           val=G[N//2].real
           if val>0:
              root=np.sqrt(val)
              if P[N//2].real < 0: root=-root
              Xt[N//2]=root
           else:
              Xt[N//2]=0.0
        P_new = (1.0-s)*P + s*Xt
        P_new[0]=1.0
        if N%2==0: P_new[N//2]=float(np.real(P_new[N//2]))
        q=np.fft.irfft(P_new,N)[:n]
        a_new=np.maximum(q,0.0)
        Snew=a_new.sum()
        if Snew < 1e-12:
            reject...
        else: a_new/=Snew
        # leakage check? compute score anyway
        sc_new,P_new2,H_new,h_new=_score_P(a_new,N)
        if not finite: reject
        if sc_new < best_sc + 1e-7:  # accept
            a_cur=a_new; P=P_new2; H=H_new; h=h_new
            if sc_new < best_sc: best_sc=sc_new; best=a_cur.copy(); print
            no=0
            s=min(0.8, s*1.03); alpha=min(0.8, alpha*1.03)
        else:
            no+=1
            s=max(0.05, s*0.85); alpha=max(0.02, alpha*0.85)
            if no > 120:
                a_cur=best.copy(); sc,P,H,h=_score_P(a_cur,N); no=0; s=s_init*0.7; alpha=alpha_init*0.7
        it+=1
    return best,best_sc
```

Now, LSE LBFGS code:
```
def _lse_fg(x, beta):
    x=np.asarray(x,float); n=x.size
    xm=x - np.max(x)
    ex=np.exp(np.clip(xm,-700,700))
    S=ex.sum()
    if S<=1e-300: return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    p=ex/S
    L=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(L); Nc=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    A=np.fft.rfft(p,N); H=A*A; h=np.fft.irfft(H,N)[:L]
    hmax=float(np.max(h))
    if hmax<=0: return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    scaled=beta*(h-hmax); scaled=np.clip(scaled,-500,500); expv=np.exp(scaled); ess=expv.sum(); if ess<=0: ...
    lse=hmax+np.log(ess)/beta
    score=2*n*lse
    w=expv/ess
    # gram = K w
    w_rev=w[::-1]
    Pf=np.fft.rfft(p,Nc); Wf=np.fft.rfft(w_rev,Nc)
    C=np.fft.irfft(Pf*Wf,Nc)
    gram = C[n-1:2*n-1]
    gram = gram[::-1]
    gp=4.0*n*gram
    mu=float(np.dot(p,gp))
    g=p*(gp-mu)
    # scale gradient maybe by 1e? To avoid huge, divide by (1+abs(score))? L-BFGS okay.
    # Ensure finite
    g=np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(score), g
```
Check slice `C[n-1:2*n-1]` length n? For n=2, L=3, L-n=1, L=3 -> C[1:3]. Good. For n=1, L=1, Nc? n-1=0, 2n-1=1. Work.

`polish_lbfgs`:

def _lbfgs_polish(a, time_budget):
   a=_norm(a); n=a.size; best=a; best_sc=_score_fast(a,_next_pow2(2*n-1))
   if best_sc==inf return
   t_end=time.monotonic()+time_budget
   x=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
   if n<=2048: betas=[100,500,2000,10000]
   elif n<=8192: betas=[200,1000,5000]
   else: betas=[500,2000]
   for beta in betas:
      if time.monotonic()>=t_end-10: break
      maxiter = int(min(2000, max(100, (t_end-time.monotonic())* (3 if n<=2048 else 1 if n<=4096 else 0.5))))
      try:
         res=minimize(_lse_fg, x, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':20})
         if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            p=_softmax(res.x); sc=_score_fast(p,_next_pow2(2*n-1)); if sc<best_sc: update; x=res.x
      except Exception: pass
   return best,best_sc
```
Need `_softmax` top-level.

But `minimize` cannot be interrupted; maxiter may still exceed time for large n. For n=8192, one iteration maybe 0.1s, maxiter 300 30s; okay. If time remaining small, maxiter small. Use `maxiter` not maxfun. L-BFGS line search may do multiple eval per iter. Could still. Use maxiter= max(50, remaining*2 for small, remaining*0.5 for large). Maybe too many. def _coordinate_shave(a, time_budget, label=""):
   a=_norm(a); n=a.size; L=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(L)
   sc,P,H,h=_score_P(a,N); h=h.copy()
   S=1.0
   best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
   t_end=...
   factors=(0.8,0.9,1.1,1.25,0.7)
   no=0; it=0
   while time<... and no<80:
      hmax=float(np.max(h)); if hmax<=0: break
      sc=2*n*hmax/(S*S)
      if sc<best_sc-1e-12: best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc; no=0
      else: no+=1
      active=np.argsort(h)[::-1][:3]
      progress=False
      for k in active:
         if time>=t_end: break
         k=int(k)
         js_start=max(0,k-n+1); js_end=min(k,n-1); mlen=js_end-js_start+1
         if mlen<=0: continue
         # choose top contributors
         js=np.arange(js_start, js_end+1)
         deriv=a[k-js]
         if mlen>6:
             part=np.argpartition(deriv, -6)[-6:]
             part=part[np.argsort(deriv[part])[::-1]]
         else:
             part=np.argsort(deriv)[::-1]

                if new <= 1e-300 or not finite: continue

                tmp[j] += delta*delta
                Snew=S+delta
                if Snew <= 1e-300: continue
                hamax=float(np.max(tmp))  # missing outside slice! Need global max outside.

```python
"""
Step-function height optimizer.

Strategy:
- Normalize sequences to sum 1, minimizing 2*n*max(conv(a,a)).
- Start from the supplied/parent construction and from several asymmetric
  low-peak priors.
- Main search: alternating projections
    time: real, nonnegative, length n, sum 1
    frequency: make FFT(a)^2 close to a low-peak target power spectrum
  The targets are asymmetric near-flat convolution profiles with controllable
  maximum, which helps escape the symmetric local minimum.
- Polish with exact coordinate descent on the largest convolution coefficients
  and with log-domain L-BFGS using a log-sum-exp approximation to max.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _next_pow2(m: int) -> int:
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _norm(a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    else:
        a = a / s
    return a


def _score_P(a: np.ndarray, N: int):
    """Score plus FFT spectrum P and convolution P^2 / h."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf, None, None, None
    L = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    H = A * A
    h = np.fft.irfft(H, N)[:L]
    h = np.real(h)
    hmax = float(np.max(h))
    if hmax < 0.0:
        hmax = 0.0
    sc = 2.0 * float(n) * hmax / (S * S)
    return sc, A, H, h


def _score_fast(a: np.ndarray, N: int) -> float:
    sc, _, _, _ = _score_P(a, N)
    return float(sc)


def _conv_self(a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    H = A * A
    return np.fft.irfft(H, N)[:L].real


def _interp_to_length(a: np.ndarray, m: int) -> np.ndarray:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = max(2, int(m))
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    if a.size == m:
        return _norm(a)
    xold = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    b = np.interp(xnew, xold, a)
    return _norm(b)


def _init_two_level(n: int, frac: float = 0.5, d: float = 1.0 / 3.0) -> np.ndarray:
    n = max(16, int(n))
    b = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    k = max(1, min(n - 1, int(frac * n)))
    d = max(0.0, min(0.99, float(d)))
    b[:k] = 1.0 + d
    b[k:] = 1.0 - d
    return _norm(b)


def _noise_init(a: np.ndarray, rng, eps: float = 0.02) -> np.ndarray:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    p = a * (1.0 + float(eps) * rng.standard_normal(a.size))
    return _norm(p)


def _shape_array(L: int, phase: float) -> np.ndarray:
    x = np.arange(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    s = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x + float(phase))
    s = s - float(s.mean())
    mx = float(s.max())
    if mx <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    s /= mx
    return s


def _flat_reference_from_shape(L: int, shape: np.ndarray, c: float) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Nonnegative, sum 1 reference profile of length L whose max is about c/L.
    For large L this corresponds to target score approximately c.
    """
    L = int(L)
    if L <= 1:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
    c = float(c)
    if c <= 1.0005:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)

    shape = np.asarray(shape, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if shape.size != L or not np.any(shape != 0.0):
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)

    mn = float(shape.min())
    amp = c - 1.0
    if mn < -1e-300:
        amp = min(amp, -1.0 / mn)
    amp = max(0.0, amp)

    ref = None
    for _ in range(30):
        base = 1.0 + amp * shape
        if float(base.min()) <= 0.0:
            amp *= 0.85
            if amp <= 1e-12:
                base = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
                break
            continue
        ref = base / base.sum()
        target_max = float(c) / float(L)
        if float(ref.max()) <= target_max * 1.03:
            return ref
        amp *= 0.92
        if amp <= 1e-12:
            return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    if ref is None:
        ref = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    return ref


def _spectral_project(a: np.ndarray, time_budget: float, phase: float,
                      min_c: float = 1.05,
                      s_init: float = 0.30,
                      alpha_init: float = 0.18,
                      label: str = ""):
    """
    Iterative projection:
      time step: keep a real nonnegative length-n sequence;
      frequency step: move FFT(a) toward a square root of a low-peak target.
    """
    a = _norm(a)
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(L)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    t_end = t0 + max(0.0, float(time_budget))

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc, best_P, best_H, best_h = _score_P(best, N)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        return best, np.inf

    a_cur = best.copy()
    P = best_P.copy()
    H = best_H
    h = best_h.copy()
    sc = best_sc

    shape = _shape_array(L, phase)
    c_target = min(2.0, max(1.1, float(best_sc) + 0.01))
    target = _flat_reference_from_shape(L, shape, c_target)

    s = float(s_init)
    alpha = float(alpha_init)
    no_imp = 0
    it = 0
    max_iters = max(200, int(max(0.0, time_budget) * 12))

    while time.monotonic() < t_end and it < max_iters:
        hsum = float(h.sum())
        if hsum <= 1e-20:
            break
        h = h / hsum
        M = float(np.max(h))
        sc = 2.0 * float(n) * M

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best = a_cur.copy()
            no_imp = 0

        # If target no longer lowers the peak, lower its nominal score c.
        tmax = float(target.max())
        if tmax >= M * 0.999:
            new_c = max(float(min_c), c_target - 0.02)
            if abs(new_c - c_target) > 1e-12:
                c_target = new_c
                target = _flat_reference_from_shape(L, shape, c_target)
                tmax = float(target.max())

        # Form target h_t. Prefer a small blend with the reference profile.
        if tmax < M * 0.999:
            ht = (1.0 - alpha) * h + alpha * target
            if float(ht.max()) >= M * 0.999:
                ok = False
                for al in (max(0.5, alpha), 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
                    ht2 = (1.0 - al) * h + al * target
                    if float(ht2.max()) < M * 0.999:
                        alpha = al
                        ht = ht2
                        ok = True
                        break
                if not ok:
                    ht = None
            else:
                ht = None if ht is None else ht
            if ht is None:
                cap = M * 0.997
                ht2 = np.minimum(h, cap)
                Sht = float(ht2.sum())
                if Sht > 0.0 and (float(ht2.max()) / Sht) < M * 0.999:
                    ht = ht2 / Sht
                else:
                    flat = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
                    ht = 0.6 * h + 0.4 * flat
        else:
            cap = M * 0.997
            ht2 = np.minimum(h, cap)
            Sht = float(ht2.sum())
            if Sht > 0.0 and (float(ht2.max()) / Sht) < M * 0.999:
                ht = ht2 / Sht
            else:
                flat = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
                ht = 0.6 * h + 0.4 * flat

        ht = np.clip(ht, 0.0, None)
        st = float(ht.sum())
        if st <= 1e-20:
            break
        ht = ht / st

        G = np.fft.rfft(ht, N)
        G[0] = 1.0 + 0.0j
        if N % 2 == 0:
            nyq = float(G[N // 2].real)
            if nyq <= 0.0:
                G[N // 2] = 0.0 + 0.0j

        # Square-root target in Fourier domain.
        Xt = np.sqrt(np.abs(G)) * np.exp(0.5j * np.angle(G))
        # Choose the square-root branch closest to current P.
        dot = Xt * np.conj(P)
        flip = np.real(dot) < 0.0
        Xt[flip] = -Xt[flip]

        Xt[0] = 1.0 + 0.0j
        if N % 2 == 0:
            gny = float(G[N // 2].real)
            if gny > 0.0:
                root = np.sqrt(gny)
                if float(P[N // 2].real) < 0.0:
                    root = -root
                Xt[N // 2] = root + 0.0j
            else:
                Xt[N // 2] = 0.0 + 0.0j

        P_new = (1.0 - s) * P + s * Xt
        P_new[0] = 1.0 + 0.0j
        if N % 2 == 0:
            P_new[N // 2] = float(np.real(P_new[N // 2])) + 0.0j

        q = np.fft.irfft(P_new, N)[:n]
        a_new = npamax = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
        Sn = float(a_new.sum())
        if Sn < 1e-12:
            no_imp += 1
            s = max(0.05, 0.85 * s)
            alpha = max(0.02, 0.85 * alpha)
            if no_imp > 150:
                a_cur = best.copy()
                sc, P, H, h = _score_P(a_cur, N)
            it += 1
            continue

        a_new = a_new / Sn
        sc_new, P_new2, H_new, h_new = _score_P(a_new, N)

        if not np.isfinite(sc_new):
            no_imp += 1
            s = max(0.05, 0.85 * s)
            alpha = max(0.02, 0.85 * alpha)
            if no_imp > 150:
                a_cur = best.copy()
                sc, P, H, h = _score_P(a_cur, N)
            it += 1
            continue

        # Mild acceptance: allow small uphill moves to escape.
        if sc_new < best_sc + 1e-7:
            a_cur = a_new
            P = P_new2
            H = H_new
            h = h_new
            sc = sc_new
            if sc_new < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_new
                best = a_cur.copy()
                if it % 200 == 0 or sc_new < best_sc + 1.0:
                    print(f"    [spec{label}] iter={it:5d} score={best_sc:.9f} budget_left={t_end-time.monotonic():6.1f}s")
            no_imp = 0
            s = min(0.80, 1.025 * s)
            alpha = min(0.80, 1.025 * alpha)
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            s = max(0.05, 0.85 * s)
            alpha = max(0.02, 0.85 * alpha)
            if no_imp > 250 and s < 0.12:
                break
            if no_imp > 150:
                a_cur = best.copy()
                sc, P, H, h = _score_P(a_cur, N)
                no_imp = 0

        it += 1

    return best, float(best_sc)


def _coordinate_shave(a: np.ndarray, time_budget: float, label: str = ""):
    """
    Exact coordinate descent on the largest convolution coefficients.
    Maintains h = a*a and current sum S without re-FFT-ing on every factor.
    """
    a = _norm(a)
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(L)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    t_end = t0 + max(0.0, float(time_budget))

    sc, _, _, h = _score_P(a, N)
    h = h.copy()
    S = 1.0
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = sc

    factors = (0.7, 0.82, 0.92, 1.12)
    no_imp = 0
    max_no = 80

    while time.monotonic() < t_end and no_imp < max_no:
        hmax = float(np.max(h))
        if hmax <= 1e-300:
            break
        sc = 2.0 * float(n) * hmax / (S * S)

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best = a.copy()
            no_imp = 0
        else:
            no_imp += 1

        active = np.argsort(h)[::-1][:3]
        progress = False

        for kval in active:
            if time.monotonic() >= t_end:
                break
            k = int(kval)
            j_start = max(0, k - n + 1)
            j_end = min(k, n - 1)
            mlen = j_end - j_start + 1
            if mlen <= 0:
                continue

            js = np.arange(j_start, j_end + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            deriv = a[k - js]

            if mlen > 6:
                part = np.argpartition(deriv, -6)[-6:]
                part = part[np.argsort(deriv[part])[::-1]]
            else:
                part = np.argsort(deriv)[::-1]

            for pos in part:
                j = int(js[pos])
                old = float(a[j])
                if old <= 1e-15:
                    continue

                sl = slice(j, 2 * n - 1 - j)
                base = h[sl].copy()
                other = a[sl - j]

                om = 0.0
                if sl.start > 0:
                    om = max(om, float(np.max(h[:sl.start])))
                if sl.stop < L:
                    om = max(om, float(np.max(h[sl.stop:])))

                best_f = None
                best_sc_j = sc
                best_delta = 0.0
                best_new = old
                best_S = S

                for f in factors:
                    new = old * float(f)
                    if new <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(new):
                        continue
                    delta = new - old
                    Snew = S + delta
                    if Snew <= 1e-300:
                        continue

                    tmp = base + 2.0 * delta * other
                    tmp[j] += delta * delta
                    cand_max = float(np.max(tmp))
                    if cand_max < om:
                        cand_max = om
                    cand_sc = 2.0 * float(n) * cand_max / (Snew * Snew)
                    if cand_sc < best_sc_j:
                        best_sc_j = cand_sc
                        best_f = f
                        best_delta = delta
                        best_new = new
                        best_S = Snew

                if best_f is not None:
                    upd = best_delta * (2.0 * other)
                    h[sl] = base + upd
                    h[sl][j] += best_delta * best_delta
                    a[j] = best_new
                    S = best_S
                    sc = best_sc_j
                    progress = True

        if not progress and no_imp > 30:
            break

    best = best / max(1e-300, float(best.sum()))
    # Recalculate exact score after normalization to reduce drift.
    best_sc = _score_fast(best, N)
    return best, best_sc


def _softmax(x: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    xm = x - float(np.max(x))
    ex = np.exp(np.clip(xm, -700.0, 700.0))
    s = float(ex.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(x.size)
    return ex / s


def _lse_fg(x, beta):
    """
    Log-sum-exp objective and gradient in log-domain.

    Variables x parameterize p_i = softmax(x)_i.  This enforces p_i >=0 and sum=1.
    """
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = x.size
    p = _softmax(x)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(L)
    Nc = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    A = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    H = A * A
    h = np.fft.irfft(H, N)[:L]
    hmax = float(np.max(h))
    if not np.isfinite(hmax) or hmax <= 0.0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    scaled = float(beta) * (h - hmax)
    scaled = np.clip(scaled, -500.0, 500.0)
    ev = np.exp(scaled)
    ess = float(ev.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ess) or ess <= 1e-300:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    LSE = hmax + float(np.log(ess)) / float(beta)
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * LSE
    w = ev / ess

    # grad of sum_k w_k h_k with respect to p: 2*Kw, where (Kw)_j=sum_i p_i w_{i+j}
    w_rev = w[::-1]
    Pf = np.fft.rfft(p, Nc)
    Wf = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, Nc)
    C = np.fft.irfft(Pf * Wf, Nc)
    gram = C[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    gram = gram[::-1]

    gp = 4.0 * float(n) * gram
    mu = float(np.dot(p, gp))
    g = p * (gp - mu)
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(score), g


def _lbfgs_polish(a: np.ndarray, time_budget: float, label: str = ""):
    """Polish with L-BFGS on log-domain LSE approximations."""
    a = _norm(a)
    n = a.size
    N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_fast(best, N)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    t_end = t0 + max(0.0, float(time_budget))

    x = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300)).astype(np.float64)

    if n <= 2048:
        betas = [100.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 10000.0]
        caps = [800, 600, 400, 250]
    elif n <= 8192:
        betas = [200.0, 1000.0, 5000.0]
        caps = [400, 250, 120]
    else:
        betas = [500.0, 2000.0]
        caps = [180, 80]

    for beta, cap in zip(betas, caps):
        if time.monotonic() >= t_end - 10:
            break
        remaining = max(0.0, t_end - time.monotonic() - 5)
        if remaining <= 5:
            break
        if n <= 2048:
            maxiter = int(min(cap, max(80, remaining * 3.0)))
        elif n <= 8192:
            maxiter = int(min(cap, max(50, remaining * 1.2)))
        else:
            maxiter = int(min(cap, max(40, remaining * 0.7)))

        try:
            res = minimize(
                _lse_fg,
                x,
                args=(beta,),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options={
                    "ftol": 1e-16,
                    "gtol": 1e-12,
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "maxls": 25,
                },
            )
            if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
                p = _softmax(res.x)
                sc = _score_fast(p, N)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best = p.copy()
                    x = res.x.astype(np.float64)
                    print(f"    [lbfgs{label}] beta={beta:.0f} score={best_sc:.9f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best, float(best_sc)


def propose_candidate(seed: int = 42, budget_s: float = 2400.0, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s)
    t_end = t_start + total - 60.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    parent_ok = False
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
            s = float(p.sum())
            if s > 0.01 and p.size >= 16:
                parent = p / s
                parent_ok = True
        except Exception:
            parent_ok = False

    # Initial incumbent.
    if parent_ok:
        best_a = parent.copy()
        best_N = _next_pow2(2 * best_a.size - 1)
        best_sc, _, _, _ = _score_P(best_a, best_N)
        print(f"Parent: n={best_a.size}, score={best_sc:.9f}")
    else:
        print("No usable parent; starting from finite two-level prior.")
        best_a = _init_two_level(2048, 0.5, 1.0 / 3.0)
        best_N = _next_pow2(2 * best_a.size - 1)
        best_sc, _, _, _ = _score_P(best_a, best_N)
        print(f"Initial two-level: n={best_a.size}, score={best_sc:.9f}")

    # Quick coordinate polish of the incumbent before large searches.
    rem = t_end - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 80:
        a_c, sc_c = _coordinate_shave(best_a, min(80.0, rem - 20.0), label="0")
        if np.isfinite(sc_c) and sc_c < best_sc:
            print(f"Initial coordinate polish improved: {best_sc:.9f} -> {sc_c:.9f}")
            best_sc = sc_c
            best_a = a_c

    # Lengths to search.  Include parent length if reasonable, but cap final practical length.
    candidate_lengths = []
    if parent_ok and 512 <= parent.size <= 8192:
        candidate_lengths.append(parent.size)
    for m in (2048, 4096, 8192):
        if m not in candidate_lengths:
            candidate_lengths.append(m)
    candidate_lengths = sorted({int(m) for m in candidate_lengths if 512 <= m <= 8192})

    phases = [np.pi / 2.0, -np.pi / 2.0, 0.0]

    # Allocate time by length: prioritize larger n, because the asymptotic score improves.
    budget_map = {}
    for m in candidate_lengths:
        if m <= 1024:
            budget_map[m] = 45.0
        elif m <= 2048:
            budget_map[m] = 75.0
        elif m <= 4096:
            budget_map[m] = 220.0
        else:
            budget_map[m] = 520.0

    for n in candidate_lengths:
        rem = t_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 40:
            break

        # Stronger allocation to the largest practical length if there is plenty of time.
        if n == max(candidate_lengths) and rem > 1200:
            n_budget = min(1050.0, rem - 250.0)
        else:
            n_budget = min(budget_map.get(n, 90.0), rem - 60.0)
        if n_budget < 35:
            continue

        # Start from the current global best if possible; otherwise use a prior.
        if best_sc < np.inf:
            a_cur = _interp_to_length(best_a, n)
            # Add a tiny asymmetric perturbation to avoid exact duplicates between lengths.
            a_cur = _noise_init(a_cur, rng, eps=0.005)
        else:
            a_cur = _init_two_level(n, 0.5, 1.0 / 3.0)

        base = _score_fast(a_cur, _next_pow2(2 * n - 1))
        print(f"n={n} start score={base:.9f}, budget={n_budget:.0f}s")

        # Spend this length budget over several phase restarts of the spectral projector.
        phase_budgets = []
        if n <= 2048:
            phase_budgets = [n_budget * 0.50, n_budget * 0.30, n_budget * 0.20]
        elif n <= 4096:
            phase_budgets = [n_budget * 0.50, n_budget * 0.30, n_budget * 0.20]
        else:
            phase_budgets = [n_budget * 0.55, n_budget * 0.275, n_budget * 0.175]

        for ph, ph_b in zip(phases, phase_budgets):
            rem = t_end - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 25:
                break
            b = min(ph_b, rem - 25.0)
            if b < 12:
                continue

            a_proj, sc_proj = _spectral_project(
                a_cur, b, ph,
                min_c=1.05,
                s_init=0.30 if n > 4096 else 0.35,
                alpha_init=0.18 if n > 4096 else 0.22,
                label=f" n={n} ph={int(round(ph*2))}"
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc_proj) and sc_proj < best_sc:
                print(f"  spectral improved n={n}: {best_sc:.9f} -> {sc_proj:.9f}")
                best_sc = sc_proj
                best_a = a_proj.copy()

            # Use the projected point, even if not globally best, as a new local basin.
            if np.isfinite(sc_proj):
                a_cur = a_proj

        # Coordinate polish after spectral search for this length.
        rem = t_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 70:
            a_coord, sc_coord = _coordinate_shave(
                a_cur,
                min(120.0 if n <= 4096 else 220.0, rem - 30.0),
                label=f" n={n}"
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc_coord) and sc_coord < best_sc:
                print(f"  coordinate improved n={n}: {best_sc:.9f} -> {sc_coord:.9f}")
                best_sc = sc_coord
                best_a = a_coord.copy()
            else:
                a_cur = a_coord

        # L-BFGS polish on smaller lengths where it is fast enough.
        if n <= 4096:
            rem = t_end - time.monotonic()
            if rem > 120:
                a_lb, sc_lb = _lbfgs_polish(a_cur, min(160.0, rem - 30.0), label=f" n={n}")
                if np.isfinite(sc_lb) and sc_lb < best_sc:
                    print(f"  lbfgs improved n={n}: {best_sc:.9f} -> {sc_lb:.9f}")
                    best_sc = sc_lb
                    best_a = a_lb.copy()

    # Final global polish.
    rem = t_end - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 100 and len(best_a) > 0:
        a_c, sc_c = _coordinate_shave(best_a, min(300.0, rem - 30.0), label="final")
        if np.isfinite(sc_c) and sc_c < best_sc:
            print(f"Final coordinate polish: {best_sc:.9f} -> {sc_c:.9f}")
            best_sc = sc_c
            best_a = a_c

    rem = t_end - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120 and len(best_a) <= 4096:
        a_lb, sc_lb = _lbfgs_polish(best_a, min(260.0, rem - 30.0), label="final")
        if np.isfinite(sc_lb) and sc_lb < best_sc:
            print(f"Final lbfgs polish: {best_sc:.9f} -> {sc_lb:.9f}")
            best_sc = sc_lb
            best_a = a_lb

    # Sanitize output.
    out = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        fallback = _init_two_level(2048, 0.5, 1.0 / 3.0)
        out = fallback
        best_sc = _score_fast(out, _next_pow2(2 * out.size - 1))

    out = out / max(1e-300, float(out.sum()))
    out = [float(x) for x in out]

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = best_sc

    print("=" * 60)
    print(f"FINAL internal={best_sc:.9f} verified={verified:.9f} n={len(out)} time={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s/{total:.0f}s")
    print("=" * 60)

    return out
```
